# META
~~~ini
description=Default and bound FeatureValues share static scalar checking: unproven numeric narrowing is a warning, unrelated result types are errors, and conforming values remain clean.
type=file
libraries=standard
require_complete_publication=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Defaults {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SequenceFunctions::size;
    calc def Text { return result : String = "text"; }
    attribute bound : Natural = 11 - 1;
    attribute defaulted : Natural default = 11 - 1;
    attribute unrelated : Natural = Text();
    attribute unrelatedDefault : Natural default = Text();
    attribute valid : Integer = 11 - 1;
    attribute validDefault : Integer default = 11 - 1;
    attribute values : Integer[1..*];
    attribute count : Positive = size(values);
}
~~~
